# Final Resolution of the Author upon philosophical contemplation of the harmonizing course of creatures in the realm of nature with the course of the human free spirit in the realm of light.

---

->Romans 11:36.<-
->*From GOD, through GOD, and to GOD are all things.*<-

After the incomprehensible original Being, through the holy and powerful Command <note>original: "Fiat" — the divine "let there be" from the Creation story in Genesis.</note> of His divine mouth, spoke forth the beginning of all creatures <note>original: "Creaturen" — in this context, referring to all created things, both living and inanimate.</note> in the form of fire and light; and also through His Spirit, which hovered upon the subtle, heavenly, and spiritual waters of this chaotic fire- and light-principle <note>original: "Principii" — the fundamental starting point or source.</note>, making the same further visible as a vapor and mist, wherein the seed of the universe <note>original: "sperma vniuerſi" — a term used in early modern natural philosophy to describe a universal life-force or substance from which all things grow.</note> or the general seed is active toward further offspring of creatures: So everything now stands in an incomparable movement, such that the spiritual descends in everlasting fermentation <note>original: "Fermentation" — here referring to a process of spiritual ripening or transformation into physical matter.</note> and becomes corporeal. Heaven and air, in their unceasing course, are constantly heated, so as to steam and sweat, and they direct their influence <note>original: "Influenz" — the traditional belief that celestial bodies and the heavens "flowed" their power down into the earthly realm.</note> downward toward water and earth. And the earth is saturated by such heavenly influence; indeed, so much so that from its abundance, through the implanted central heat <note>original: "Central-Hitze" — a concept in early science and alchemy suggesting an internal fire at the heart of the earth that drives the growth of minerals and plants.</note>, it resolves <note>original: "reſolviret" — meaning to dissolve or break down a solid back into its basic elements.</note> the corporeal back into steam and vapor and sends it backward toward its first origin. What an excellent alternation of ascending and descending the Creator has implanted out of the wisest order of nature, to continue <note>original: "continuiren"</note> this until He [judges] the world according to

<meta>catchword: his (feinem)</meta>

<summary>The author begins a philosophical conclusion, describing the creation of the world from light and fire and detailing a cyclical process where spiritual forces become physical matter and then return to their source through "central heat."</summary>
<keywords>Creation, Fiat, natural philosophy, alchemy, central heat, spiritual fermentation, cosmology</keywords>

will bring an end to His will. In this general course of all creatures, I have also perceived that the more the spiritual descends into the corporeal, the more such corporeal-become things seek to satisfy themselves anew from their origin, namely from the heavenly. And where a thing is removed too far from this heavenly influence <term>Influenz: from the Latin "influere," meaning a flowing in of celestial or divine power into the physical world</term> through deep confinement in the bonds of the earthly, it finally becomes a completely hungry <term>terra damnata: "condemned earth"; in alchemy, this refers to the useless, dry ash or residue remaining after all "spirit" and "soul" have been extracted from a substance</term>, wherein it lies in its dark hunger for so long and, as it were, suffers in anguish, until it is perhaps seized by a new influence of light, rids itself of the earthly dark bonds, and can gradually rise again to its origin.

Here now, in this noble course of creatures, I have perceived a beautiful image of that blessed course which the most noble creature—the free spirit of man—must certainly observe if he wishes to attain his true peace. For that his origin is from **GOD** is well known from the Book of Creation. But that he must, in his course, constantly satisfy and refresh himself for his tranquility through the heavenly influence from **GOD**, which consists in the enjoyment of divine love, and to that end must unceasingly "imagine" <note>original: "imaginiren" — in the mystical tradition of Jakob Böhme, this refers to the soul's power to focus its desire and "form" itself into the image of what it loves.</note> in his love-desire into **GOD** as his origin, if he is to maintain true peace within himself: This is indeed to be seen from the fall of the first man, who sank himself into a thousand restless desires for the earthly and placed himself and his descendants into a languishing hunger of the free spirit, after this free spirit had once ceased to satisfy itself solely on the heavenly love-influence of **GOD** through its imagination. Here he lay, as it were, locked in his <term>terra damnata: here used metaphorically for the spiritually desolate state of humanity after the Fall</term> as if in dark bonds of earthly worldly-love, the burning fire of his desires

And this unrest of the poor spirit departed from **GOD** lasts as long as until the love of **GOD** in Christ seizes this spirit of man anew, and then leads it back into its true origin.

Hier I became aware how miserable and futile—indeed, truly lamentable—all the actions of men are, as long as they are not in a constant elevation of their spirit toward their original essence. For after I began to observe all kinds of human minds and their occupations with greater attention, I always perceived something different in each; however, when I looked quite diligently and especially awaited the end <note>The "end" here likely refers to the final outcome of these lives or the state of the soul at the moment of death.</note> of many, I found that they had mostly all determined themselves toward one purpose, namely toward a sophistical <term>sophistisch: in this mystical context, it refers to a false or deceptive wisdom that appears intellectually clever but lacks spiritual truth</term> and futile essence. This contemplation finally led me to begin reflecting on my own efforts as well, and I examined them according to the touchstone <term>Probier-Stein: literally a "testing stone" used to check the purity of gold or silver; here, a metaphor for a spiritual standard of truth</term> of divine light. There I saw how I had spent half of my years with unnecessary worries, futile arrangements, and other things burdensome to the spirit. I thought myself wise enough to undertake the most difficult matters, and everything had to be made possible through my arrangement. But all these things, though they may be regarded as praiseworthy in human eyes, I now find to be like fetters which many times kept my spirit captive in vain love of the world and the flesh, so that it could not return to its origin.

I am therefore now beginning to see with entirely different eyes, to hear with different ears, and to speak with a different mouth. In short, I am learning to separate the pure from the impure <note>original Latin: *purum ab impuro*</note>, the light from the darkness, and the blessing from the curse through **GOD'S** grace. For I perceive how all the efforts of men outside of **GOD**, and all vain knowledge leading away from this divine origin, belong to the Tree of Knowledge, through which the curse is stirred and all impure corruption is mixed into the creature.

From this I now wish to depart: to renounce all my desires for science and knowledge which scattered my mind and dragged it away from the path of simplicity <term>Einfalt: in mystical thought, this is "singleness of heart," a state of being focused solely on God rather than the "multiplicity" of the world</term> toward the path of multiplicity, and in such a way allow myself to be led ever closer to my center and point of rest in **GOD**. Accordingly, through the grace of the divine light, I have formed an entirely different idea of true wisdom. For this heavenly Sophia <note>The Greek word for Wisdom, often personified in mystical literature as a divine feminine bride of the soul or the reflected mirror of God's glory.</note>, who first truly unlocks the understanding of man, fills it with divine light, and is communicated by **GOD** out of grace from above through the Spirit of Wisdom—and who must certainly come upon all who walk in the simplicity of the spirit—this Sophia, I say, can thus never be attained through human art and

And in my life I have often been very saddened by the miserable connection of those "school-truths" and by the wretched systematic structures of a so-called doctrine of wisdom, such as is presented from the lectern, whereby young minds are often more corrupted than edified, and perhaps even by means of which atheistic principles <note>original Latin: *principia*</note> are inspired in them. I have, alas, myself tested and tasted these vain shells of wisdom, but found no kernel, and thus neither strength nor life-sap within them. And at last I believed I had found the cause of why such turgid, worthless wisdom, devoid of strength and life, arises. Namely, I saw that commonly the conceited Doctors of Philosophy <note>original Latin: *Doctores philosophiæ*</note> in their peddling of wisdom indeed begin with the knowledge of all things; but they seldom tend to arrive at the knowledge of ourselves with proper solidity <note>original: *Solidité*</note>, much less have they derived that which they have recognized from their own *practical* experience—which is often immediately cried down as *enthusiastic* <term>enthusiastisch: in the 17th and 18th centuries, "enthusiasm" was a derogatory label for religious radicals who claimed to receive direct, emotional revelations from God rather than through scripture or church authority</term>—but instead have derived it only from mere theoretical and poorly connected opinions. All of which, however, only prevents the true access of divine wisdom to our heart in a distressing way. Oh, we should seek our greatest wisdom solely in this: that we learn to know **GOD** and ourselves in the divine light; whereas all other knowledge should serve, as it were, as a guide to seek the Creator and to strive for true union with **GOD**. Oh, if there were not a wisdom that led man onto these blissful paths, then indeed a man, who with all his

Since indeed such a lifeless creature—because it is not capable of making reflections <note>original: *reflexiones*</note> as humans do—on this very basis is not nearly as able to offend us as we humans, on the other hand, through our many vain knowings, rationalizing, and reflecting, can be misled into offending the stones: using them for the service of vanity and lust, and as it were compelling them so that they must, in a certain way, sigh to their Creator about these vain men. <note>An allusion to the biblical concept in Romans 8:22, where "the whole creation groaneth and travaileth in pain together."</note>

This is because the free spirit of man—which perhaps for the whole duration of life has engaged in no overly serious struggle against the flesh, nor sought to cast off its bonds—then finds the final struggle all the more significant and serious. For since we must give a very exact account of everything we owe to God, ourselves, and our neighbor: so indeed in such a final moment of life, a man must break through with great power <note>original: *force*</note> of faith, if he does not wish to enter from temporality into eternity as a wretched slave in such bonds of the flesh. In such wrestling struggles of the spirit, all human cleverness, understanding, and wisdom, all superfluous reading <note>original: *Lecture*</note>, erudition, and yes, all charlatanry <note>original: *Charlatanerie*; here meaning empty intellectual pretension</note> ceases at once. # Second Part of the Theosophical Heart-Devotions,

->Consisting<-
->**In various reflections on the core and quintessence <term>Quint-Essenz: In alchemy and early modern philosophy, the "fifth element" or the purest, most essential part of a substance.</term> of true divine wisdom.**<-

---

## The First Reflection.
### On the Restoration of the Corrupted Creature in Three Divine Dispensations <term>Haushaltungen: Literally "households" or "managements," here referring to the theological concept of "dispensations"—the specific ways God governs and relates to humanity during different eras of salvation history.</term>.

---

->Hosea 6:2 <note>original: "Hos. 6, 2."</note><-

> After two days he will revive us; on the third day he will restore us, that we may live in his presence.

<image-desc>A small crown ornament centered above the main text block.</image-desc>

<image-desc>A large decorative initial 'G' framed within an ornate shield. Atop the shield sits a dove holding a branch in its beak.</image-desc>

Great JEHOVAH, you call yourself in the Old Testament—where you revealed yourself most clearly as Father—a consuming fire, Deuteronomy 4:24 <note>original: "5. B. Mos. 4, 24." In German tradition, Deuteronomy is referred to as the 5th Book of Moses.</note>.

<meta>catchword: There</meta>

<summary>This page serves as the title page and opening for the second part of the devotional work, introducing a series of reflections on the restoration of humanity through three stages of divine history. It opens with a scriptural foundation from Hosea and an invocation of God as a "consuming fire."</summary>

<keywords>Theosophy, Restoration, Divine Wisdom, Dispensations, Hosea 6:2, Deuteronomy, Consuming Fire, Divine Fatherhood</keywords>

<meta>This page continues the "First Reflection" from page 84, shifting the focus from God the Father as a "consuming fire" in the Old Testament to the manifestation of the Son as "Light" in the New Testament. <header>❧ ) ☼ ( ❧</header>

Whereas your most beloved Son, when he revealed himself in the flesh in the New Testament, is called *a light* (John 1:9 <note>original: "Joh. 1, v. 9."</note>), indeed, he even gives himself this name (John 12:46 <note>original: "Joh. 12. v. 46."</note>). And so I recognize in deepest humility that you, according to the characteristic of the Father, are to be considered <term>consideriren: To contemplate or view; a Germanized form of the Latin *considerare*.</term> as a fire, whose burning desire has moved from eternity as an eternal root of creation, to bring forth from your heart the divine, gentle light- and love-essence of your Son—the delight and good pleasure of your will. This divine spiritual fire and light of your eternal will-to-love finally became active in the creation of all things through the Spirit emanating from both. This, your divine Spirit, has thus carried out the active power of your divine will-to-love, or the fire- and light-principle <term>principii: The Latin genitive of *principium*, meaning the fundamental source, origin, or beginning.</term> of your eternal nature, and depicted it in the fire- and light-form of the outer visible nature.

Just as you also maintained this order of creation in the spiritual world of the inner man—where you likewise completed everything from the fire, through the light, into the Spirit—so you continue in the same order to bring back your fallen creature and to restore it, as it were, through a new creation. Here too, your Godhead wishes to reveal itself in the Trinity and to carry out the work of restoration <term>Wiederbringung: A central concept in German theosophy (often called *Apokatastasis*), referring to the process of returning the soul or the world to its original, perfect state in God.</term> in three-fold operations. For here also I perceive how the fire of your divine will-desire is active in your characteristic as Father, to draw souls through the fire of anguish <term>Angst: In this mystical context, *Angst* refers to a state of spiritual "narrowness" or painful contraction—the soul's suffering under the Law before it is liberated by grace.</term> and contrary sensation under the Law, so that they may thirst for the eternal righteousness, or for the light- and love-essence in your Son; so that he may refresh them in the love and gentleness of his pleasant light, and [quench] the thirsting fire of the burning desire for anguish and will from the first principle <term>principio: Referring to the first of the three "principles" or foundations of existence, which represents the dark, fiery power of God's strength.</term> in them

<meta>catchword: through</meta>

<summary>This page explores the Trinitarian nature of creation and salvation, contrasting the Father's "fire" with the Son's "light." It explains that the spiritual restoration of a person follows the same cosmic order as the original creation: moving from the fire of the Law and spiritual anguish into the light of Christ's love.</summary>
<keywords>Trinity, Father as Fire, Son as Light, Restoration, inner man, spiritual anguish, the Law, first principle, new creation, Böhme-influenced theology</keywords>

It develops the concept of the "Three Economies" (Father, Son, and Spirit) as both an internal psychological process of rebirth and a historical progression from the Old Testament Law to the New Testament Gospel.</meta>

<page-num>86</page-num>
<header>) ✺ (</header>

...ration, <note>This completes the word "restoration" begun on the previous page.</note> so that he may soothe it through the introduced light. This divine light and fire is then the spiritual light-body in the new creature, which is brought forth to birth by the Holy Spirit and adorned and perfected with various gifts and powers.

Permit me, most gracious Father, to imagine this working in the task of the new creation under the image of three households, and to call these the economies <term>Oeconomien: From the Greek *oikonomia*, referring to a divine plan or a specific stage in the administration of God's work on earth.</term> of the Father, Son, and Holy Spirit. In these, your divine Fatherly power takes the corrupted creature in hand once more in its will-desire according to the first economy and speaks: *Let us make an image that is like us.* You then hand such a creature over to your Son for a new quickening <note>quickening: the act of bringing to life or making "alive."</note> in the quality of light according to the second economy, so that finally the Holy Spirit, in the third economy, may complete the full birth of the new creature.

Regarding this threefold household, which you observe in the restoration of every person, you have also allowed us to see a beautiful image in that threefold economy of your outwardly-guided kingdom of grace on earth. For just as you conducted the entire household in such an outer kingdom of yours under Moses and under the Law, according to your Fatherly characteristic in the first principle <term>principio: The fundamental source or beginning; a Latin term commonly used in mystical philosophy to describe the different "modes" of God's existence.</term> of fire—even descending with fire upon the mountain at the proclamation of your divine law, *Exodus 19:18* <note>original: "2. B. Mos. 19, 18." The "Second Book of Moses" is the German name for Exodus.</note>—so that the appearance of your glory was like a consuming fire; *Exodus 24:17.* <note>original: "2 B. Mos. 24, 17."</note>

Conversely, you began a new economy under Christ in the New Covenant, where the lost treasures of light and salvation were regained and acquired; therefore, this economy may certainly be called the economy of the Son, according to the second principle of light, in which your Son himself was the light that shines into the darkness—

It describes the restoration of the soul as a process where the Father initiates, the Son quickens with light, and the Holy Spirit completes the birth of a "new creature."</summary>

<page-num>87</page-num>
<header>-> * ) ( * <-</header>
<meta>catchword: Cov- <note>German: Bun- (for "Bund," meaning covenant)</note></meta>

...[shi]ne, <note>Completing the word *scheinen* (to shine) from the previous page.</note> *John 1:5,* and those who received it were called children of light: *Ephesians 5:9.* <note>The author cites verse 9, though "Children of Light" appears in Ephesians 5:8.</note> So also now the time is dawning when, according to the pronouncement of the Holy Scriptures, the workings of grace of your good and Holy Spirit will be powerful for the expansion of your kingdom among Jews and Gentiles, to make known and share the blessings of salvation <term>Heils-Güter: The spiritual benefits or "goods" obtained through Christ's redemption.</term> throughout the whole world—blessings to which the Father called in the first economy, and which the Son acquired in the second economy. Dear Father, even in your written Word I find various traces of this threefold household in your kingdom. For when you speak there in *Exodus 19:10–11* to Moses: *Go to the people and sanctify them today and tomorrow, let them wash their clothes and be ready for the third day. For on the third day the Lord will come down before the eyes of all the people;* it seems to me as if a great mystery <note>original: *Geheimniß*</note> lies in these divine words of yours, and that perhaps something is contained herein of those wonderful things and of the secrets of future times, which you revealed to Moses on Mount Sinai according to the account in *4 Esdras 14:4–5.* <note>4 Esdras is an apocalyptic book found in many German Bibles of this era, though now usually placed in the Apocrypha.</note>

Most certainly, you, all-wise Father, according to the holy triad <note>original: *Dreyzahl*</note> of your Godhead, and especially according to its characteristics, offices, and workings, have indicated three distinct days or rather times, which follow one after another and are assigned as distinct households <term>Haushaltungen: Administrations or "economies" of divine history.</term> in your outward general kingdom to the Father, the Son, and the Holy Spirit. Was not the time of the Law in the Old Testament the time and the day of the Father, when you conducted your household in your kingdom according to the Father's characteristic, and were always occupied in bringing people to your Son Christ through various laws and prefigurations <note>original: *Vorbilder*</note>? On the other hand, in the New...

# The Second Contemplation.
## *Concerning the Restoration of the Corrupted Creature, and specifically under the Economy of the Father, according to the first Principle of Fire.*

---

> John 6:44.
> No one can come to me unless the Father, who sent me, draws him.

**D**earest Father, everything that you have undertaken with us poor humans from the beginning of the world, and which even seems to have happened in your wrath, is nonetheless pure love in the end, and determines itself

...toward the restoration of our salvation. For though we had not so quickly deviated with our will's desire from the light-essence <note>original: *Licht-VVesen*; referring to the spiritual substance of God's love.</note> of your divine love, the kingdom of light immediately withdrew itself from man in the world, and in contrast, the kingdom of darkness and wrath revealed itself within him of its own accord. But this very feeling of your divine wrath was to be, in turn, the beginning of the restoration of your corrupted creatures. Here, according to your nature as Father, you wished to begin your administration <term>Haushaltung: A "household management" or era of divine history; here referring specifically to the first era of God's dealings with humanity.</term> in the fire of an adverse sensation of your divine wrath, so that this strict fire-impulse <note>original: *Feuer-Trieb*; a term from Böhmean theosophy describing the raw, driving energy of the divine or human will before it is softened by light.</note> of your divine will might once again powerfully draw our free spirit, which had strayed from the light, and bring it into the fellowship of the light.

Oh, this powerful pull within the fire manifested itself in this administration of yours in a very hidden way. For when you wished to pull most strongly, you seemed to abandon us entirely and cast us quite deeply into the fire of our own will, which had gone out from the light. The strict fire-will <note>original: *Feuer-Wille*</note> of man, which otherwise found its true rest in the divine light of your Son, nevertheless desired to be soothed and to have food for its refreshment since it had lost this light. Therefore, it sought a light in this world among temporal pleasures; but that was transitory. Thus, it could in no way satisfy the eternal fire-desire of our will, being but a transitory food. 

Here now, dear Father, your grace-filled pull <note>original: *Gnaden-voller Zug*; referring back to the "drawing" mentioned in John 6:44 on the previous page.</note> toward the light of your Son revealed itself, in that you—so to speak—surrendered us to the fire of our own spirit, which had deviated from the light and fixed its imagination <note>original: *imaginirenden*; in this context, it means the spirit shaping itself or focusing its desires upon an object.</note> into the center of this world as into a false earthly light; a spirit which indeed seeks to satisfy the severity of its desires in the earthly goods of this world, in the lust of the eyes,

...to seek rest in the air of the flesh <note>original: *Fleisches-Luft*; likely a variation of *Fleischeslust* (lust of the flesh), suggesting the spiritual atmosphere or "breath" of carnal desire.</note>, and in a haughty life; but it finds ground and foundation for true rest nowhere. Instead, the more the desires immerse themselves in earthly things, the deeper the abyss they find for sinking, and the false, evil hunger for the earthly light grows all the more in the abundance of the earthly air and is never stilled therein. 

Great GOD, how deep Your wisdom <note>original: *VVeisheit*</note> goes here! This languishing hunger of the spirit, which finds no right, pleasing, or lasting satisfaction in the enjoyment of the creature <note>referring to created things or "the creation" rather than the Creator</note>, serves You, as it were, as a hidden preparation of the heart, so that it might afterwards surrender itself all the more willingly to the fiery pull of Your Father-property <term>Vaters-Eigenschaft: In theosophical thought (specifically Böhmean), this refers to the stern, just, and "fiery" attribute of God associated with the Law, which must be experienced before one can reach the "Light" or "Love" of the Son.</term> toward the noble essence of light and love of Your Son. For when the degenerate fire of our desires stands in its fiercest hunger, the pure fire of Your law stirs within our conscience with an adverse sensation of anxiety and Your divine wrath, which is revealed in the law, and intends to consume and, as it were, burn away the corrupted fire of our self-will and that life which seeks to maintain itself in external nature.

As a representation of how You walk this fire-path in the law with every fallen creature for its restoration, You also conducted this economy <term>Oeconomie: From the Greek *oikonomia*, meaning "household management"; here referring to God’s plan or era of spiritual administration in history.</term> according to Your Father-property in Your outward kingdom under Moses. Abraham, the father of the old covenant, even had to begin this outward administration of Yours through circumcision, before Isaac, the son of his love and the true heir of his goods, was born to him. He came out of Ur in Chaldea, which according to its name signifies a fire <note>The Hebrew name *Ur* (אוּר) translates literally to "fire" or "flame."</note>. And in this image, You let us wonderfully recognize how You set out in the fire of Your earnest and strict will to work the circumcision of the self-willed human...

[existence] in external nature, so that the revelation of the Son of Love, as the true heavenly Isaac <note>In this context, Isaac is a "type" or prophetic precursor to Christ (the "Son of Love"), representing the child of promise and sacrifice.</note>, might no longer be held back within human souls. Indeed, when Your servant Moses was to first put this divine economy <term>Haushaltung / Oeconomie: A theological term referring to God's "management" or "dispensation" of human history in stages, such as the Age of the Law followed by the Age of Grace.</term> of Yours under the Law into right order, You showed him beforehand—in the image of the burning bush on Mount Sinai (Exodus 3:2)—how You wished this economy to be conducted.

Was the thornbush here not an image of the human spirit standing in its deviation <note>meaning the soul's turn away from God's original order.</note>? For *the best among men is, after the Fall, like a thorn, and the most upright like a hedge* (Micah 7:4).

But this thornbush stands upon the Mountain of the Law, Sinai, and is thus subject to the Law; and the fire of Your divine wrath reveals itself in the Law to ignite the thornbush of the deviated human spirit, and to dispose it so that it might gladly and willingly sacrifice all ungodly and unholy essence of sinful self-will <term>Eigenwillen: In German mystical thought, this is the "self-will" or ego that acts for itself rather than for God; it must be "broken" or "sacrificed."</term> to this heavenly fire, which proceeds from You and Your divine holiness and righteousness. 

Although in this economy the thornbush of the evil self-will indeed burns in the fire of Your wrath, yet it is not consumed; that is, the self-will is indeed humbled significantly in this fire, so that it begins to hate its thorny and stinging nature and to desire the gentle light of a quiet essence of love. But the fulfillment is not yet there, nor will it be reached until You have brought us, through Your fiery Fatherly-pull <note>original: *Vaters-Zug*; a Pietist term for the inward, irresistible drawing of the soul by God the Father toward the Son.</note>, into the other economy of the Son in the Light.

All sacrificial animals in this Your dispensation under the Law had to be slaughtered and mostly burned with fire. And by this, You intended to show mankind that all animal-like life <note>original: *thierische Leben*; referring to the base, instinctual, or carnal nature of fallen humanity.</note> which seeks only to maintain itself in external nature—

This Lamb might lead them, according to the Gospel, into the Sanctuary, so that there they may be enlightened by His light, purified in His blood, and nourished in the hidden man <note>The "inner" or "spiritual" self.</note> by the showbread <note>The sacred bread kept in the biblical Tabernacle, representing God's presence and provision.</note> of the Sanctuary—by the bread that has come from heaven. Thus, they may find again the true life of the Spirit, which they lost through the perishable food of this world, at Your heavenly table within the Light- and Love-essence of Your Son.

Yet the wonders of Your wisdom are far from being all discovered here. Also in the realm of nature, You have walked precisely such paths, by means of which You came to the aid of the suffering creatures after the Fall, into which the curse has mixed itself...

that you, dear Father, through Christ in the Holy Spirit, kindle in the innermost depths of the heart among eager souls, is full of power and life, destroys the realm of sin and hell, makes one newborn in heart, courage, and mind, reveals Christ in the soul—indeed, it is the **Body of Power** <term>Kraft-Leib</term> <note>original: "Kraft-Leib"; a term from the mystical tradition of Jakob Böhme referring to a spiritual, "substantial" body that the believer receives from Christ, contrasting with the mortal, fleshly body</note> of Christ himself, with which the soul is essentially clothed: heavenly fire and light, or Light and Justice <note>An allusion to the "Urim and Thummim," the sacred objects used by Israelite priests; in German Bibles of this era, these were often translated as "Licht und Recht"</note>, the true, heavenly **Philosopher’s Stone**, which performs nothing but wonders and opens all the secrets of divine miracles.

Yet, most kind Father, your wisest ways proceed in pleasant harmony in both the spiritual and physical worlds, and the realm of nature remains forever a mirror and an image of that which you have done—or will yet do—in the realm of grace within the inner man, as well as in the outward assembly of the faithful from the beginning of the world until the end, according to your three **dispensations** <term>Haushaltungen</term> <note>original: "Haushaltungen"; a theological concept where God governs the world through different "households" or eras, typically those of the Father, the Son, and the Holy Spirit</note>. The hungry **condemned earth** <term>terra damnata</term> <note>original: "terra damnata"; a Latin alchemical term for the worthless, dry residue left after distillation. Here, it represents the "dark" material world that still hungers for spiritual light</note>, acting as a dark, attracting magnet in the center of the earth, shall now give us further opportunity to linger a while longer in simplest admiration of this, your wisdom. Here, it metaphors the human condition after the Fall.</note>. Here the free spirit likewise thirsts, like a dark magnet in its harsh and burning fire-hunger, for a soothing and refreshment in the light. 

This divine and heavenly light is offered to it and is very near for its calming. For the Scripture says: *The word is near you, namely in your mouth and in your heart. Romans 10:8.* But because the dark magnet of the corrupted will has turned itself outward in its fire-desire—into the world, into earthly things, indeed into its own self—and seeks its light or its Christ now in the depths of carnal lust and the lust of the eyes, now in the heights of a proud life, or vain wisdom, knowledge, and puffing-up science <note>referencing Romans 10:6-7; the author warns against seeking spiritual truth through intellectual pride or sensory pleasure</note>, its strict and dark fire-desire closes itself all the more firmly within its interior with fierce hunger. It holds the hidden **spark** <note>original: "Füncklein"; a reference to the "Seelenfünklein" or "spark of the soul," a term used by mystics like Meister Eckhart to describe the indestructible essence of God within a person</note> of the true light captive, so that it cannot break forth into a budding and life-giving power. 

Most kind Father, teach me to recognize that both in the realm of nature and grace, *the true divine light—from which all divine and natural wisdom comes, and with it all good things and countless riches, indeed, joy in all things (Book of Wisdom 7:10, 11, 12)*—that, I say, such light is certainly not obtained from without if one merely woos the treasures of wisdom in carnal desire and, by means of them, yearns with vain intentions for the calming of a corrupted desire of the will. ## The Third Meditation.
### *Regarding the Restoration of the Corrupted Creature, under the Dispensation of the Son according to the Second Principle of Light.*

->John 1:4-5<-

O my God, how you strive with your apostate creatures. You carry us like sick children from one bed to another, and as it were, double your care for us daily. When we first, under your fatherly care in the fire of your divine Law, become truly aware of our sickness, you then deliver us into the hands of our true Physician and into the **dispensation** <term>Oeconomie</term> <note>Referring to the "Economy of the Son," the second stage of divine interaction with humanity focused on redemption through Christ.</note> of your Son in the Light. ### *Concerning the restoration of the corrupted creature, under the dispensation of the Son according to the second Principle of Light.*

->John 1:4, 5.<-

> *In him was life, and the life was the light of men. When we first, under your fatherly care, truly realize our illness within the fire of your divine Law, you then hand us over into the hands of our true Physician and into the **dispensation** <term>Oeconomie</term> <note>Referring to the "Divine Economy," the way God manages the salvation of humanity through different stages or persons of the Trinity.</note> of your Son in the light. And when this faithful Helper reveals himself with his **tincture of love** <term>Liebes-Tinctur</term> <note>An alchemical term used by Jakob Böhme and his followers to describe the spiritual essence or "medicine" of Christ that transforms the "base metal" of the fallen soul into "spiritual gold."</note>, when this soothing light breaks through from the dark ground of the heart, then the hungry magnet of our corrupted **self-will** <term>Eigenvvillen</term> must shatter and fall away into a dark, black earth of death. ...the souls are **tinctured** <term>tingiret</term> <note>An alchemical term used to describe a spiritual transformation or "dyeing" of the soul's essence.</note> again, the fire of wrath is soothed under the painful sensation of the Law, and the head of the monstrous serpent of the self-willed life in man is crushed. <note>A reference to the "protoevangelium" in Genesis 3:15, where God promises that the offspring of the woman will crush the serpent's head.</note>

O sweetest JESUS, how lovely and comforting you have conducted this **dispensation** <term>Haushaltung</term> in your external kingdom of grace, when you appeared visibly in the world and, in the days of your flesh, performed externally in your suffering that which you still perform spiritually in all people. Your birth and meritorious suffering, through which you, as the Light of the World, prepared a way to us humans to enlighten them in their darkness and to once again share with them the true light-life—yes, you have fully depicted your entire **dispensation** <term>Haushaltung</term> in the property of Light through the person of Isaac. For just as Abraham represented the **dispensation** under the Law in the **fiery property** <term>Feuer-Eigenschaft</term> of the Father: so now the son of Abraham is an Isaac, that is, a "Son of Joy," <note>The name Isaac (Yitzhak) in Hebrew means "he will laugh" or "laughter."</note> to show that you, most beloved Lord JESUS, are a Son of Love to your heavenly Father, and the delight and pleasure of his will, the gentle light of joy and love in which the fire of his divine will is soothed and becomes entirely joyful for the refreshment of those poor consciences who, outside of your divine light, languished under the strict fiery wrath of the Father. Ah, how delightful, furthermore, is the story of Isaac in his sacrifice. Abraham and Isaac go together and wish to represent the **restoration** <term>Wiederbringung</term> of humanity as living images. Isaac had placed himself under the burden of the wood for the burnt offering, to show how you, my JESUS, as the divine Light- and Love-Being, revealed yourself in our flesh, joining yourself with the corruptible, natural, and mortal man as if with the fragile, combustible wood of your body

to be clothed over and, as it were, to be burdened with its destructive load. For although you were not conceived and born in sin, and thus had no sin of your own: nevertheless, this body of yours was taken from our stained external nature, which still lies under the curse. Here, therefore, dear Savior <note>original: "Heyland"</note>, you bore our sickness in your body under the image of the wood (Isaiah 53:4) and desired to sacrifice our sin in your body in the fire of God’s wrath (1 Peter 4:24 <note>The original text cites 1 Pet 4:24, though it likely intends 1 Peter 2:24: "Who his own self bare our sins in his own body on the tree."</note>). Abraham, the image of your heavenly Father, carried the fire with which he intended to light the wood.

Therefore you, dearest **JESUS**, walked in the likeness of sinful flesh and blood, so that in your suffering you might offer up this combustible wood to the fire of divine wrath as a sacrifice, and thus pave a way to lead the life of the external man into death—that life which, like a dark earth lying under the curse, still holds your divine light back in unrighteousness and shuts it up in its dark bonds in all fallen creatures. 

Oh, wisest and most unsearchable **GOD**, your ways are full of mysteries, and you play out the restoration through light and fire even in living persons. Just as you have also depicted it in the kingdom of nature through the elementary light enclosed within the dark magnet <note>In theosophical thought, the "magnet" refers to the "First Property of Nature," a spiritual force of contraction and desire that traps light in darkness.</note>, which is awakened by a pure fire to break through the bonds of the dark magnet or a hungry <term>damned earth</term> <note>original: "terra damnata." In alchemy, this refers to the worthless ash or residue remaining after a substance has been purified; here it symbolizes the fallen, material state of humanity.</note>, and, by means of a pure fire, to bring this dark, harsh fire of an earth contracting in hunger completely into the death of a total destruction.

<meta>This page continues the allegorical interpretation of the Binding of Isaac. It transitions from the imagery of the "wood" (human nature) to the "ram" (Christ’s external humanity) and discusses how Christ participated in fallen nature without himself becoming a sinner.</meta>

<page-num>100</page-num>

Isaac lay bound upon the wood, and Abraham stood before him, armed with fire and knife, ready to slaughter and to burn. This is once again a representation <note>original: "Vorstellung"—a mental picture or spiritual type</note> of how you, my Love, came into our flesh which lies under the curse, and how you were surrounded, as it were, by the fire of divine wrath. It shows how the heavenly Abraham, with the sword of his earnest fire-will, pressed powerfully upon the **external man** <note>In mystical theology, the "external man" is the physical, earthly part of a person, often contrasted with the "internal" spiritual soul.</note>, in which you, my Light, lay as if bound and locked away, and demanded nothing but destruction and death for that external part. Finally, the ram hanging in the thicket by its horns was slaughtered. Through this, Isaac was freed from his bonds, so that he could help the Father complete the sacrifice of the ram.

Dearest Love, most precious Lord **JESUS**, this ram is also an image of your external humanity, of which we can say with truth that it indeed remained hanging in the thorn-thicket of the universal curse that oppressed the entire human race. Oh, far be it from me, most holy Lord **JESUS**, that I would wish to make you into a sinner through these expressions. For it is a very different thing to have sin within oneself—and that with pleasure and consent—and, on the other hand, to bear the sin of others as a burden and to feel the curse of others' sins within oneself through many struggles and wrestlings. 

And so it was certainly the case with you, my Savior <note>original: "Heyland"</note>. For although your birth and conception from the holy Virgin did not happen in sin, as our birth does: nevertheless, the veil of your flesh in external nature was taken from the very flesh and blood which still lay under the Fall and the curse. Thus, in this external body of yours, you were subject to all those infirmities arising from the Fall—such as hunger, thirst, frost, and heat—and indeed all temptations, yet without consent and without sin,

Therefore, wherever water and air meet a substance, the putrefaction of that substance is promoted first. For it is precisely that pure spiritual fire and light lying hidden in water and air which is the warming moisture of nature; through its gentle fermenting power, it sets everything in which it works into death and rot, and tears open the bonds from the inside out, in which this subtle, spiritual, and fermenting power of light and fire lay locked away. calls it, which we, who have sunk into a deep earthly darkness, could no longer have grasped in its spiritual nature and clarity, even though it shone in our darkness. *John 1:5.* Therefore, it was necessary that you, O incomprehensible Love, became physical and took on our flesh, so that we poor humans lying in earthly bonds might see you clothed in our physical form, and recognize your divine glory, which consists in grace and truth—that is, in light and **justice** <note>original: *Recht*—used here as a synonym for divine order or righteousness, paralleling "truth" in John 1:14.</note>, or to speak according to our **principles** <note>original: *principiis*</note>, in light and fire—and might embrace it as the true food in our **desire of faith** <term>desire of faith: the spiritual hunger or orientation of the soul toward God</term>. *John 1:14.*

<meta>This page continues the author’s philosophical investigation into why spiritual forces must take on physical "clothing." <page-num>108</page-num>

...heavenly light enclosed within the waters, or *Majim* <note>original: *Majim*—Hebrew for "waters," often used in a mystical context to refer to the primordial or celestial waters</note>, of the female seed, had appeared to them anew. Without this outward, physical clothing, it could not be grasped or comprehended by the earthly creatures standing in parched hunger, because of its spirituality. Rather, this subtle essence of light and fire would quickly scatter again without air and water; indeed, it would pull the volatile essence of our natural spirit—which still lies in earthly bonds—or the *anima sensitiua* <note>Latin: "sensitive soul," the animal spirit responsible for sensation and movement in the body</note> itself out of the dark body after it. For this **Archaeus** <term>Archaeus: in Paracelsian medicine, the hidden vital force or "internal chemist" that governs the functions of an organ or the whole body</term> or *anima sensitiua* lives upon light and fire as its proper food, but cannot receive such food in the earthly dark body without air and water. This is felt more than enough on high mountains, where air begins to fail. Humans and livestock, indeed everything in and on the earth, thirst for water, and for its **conservation** <note>maintenance or preservation of life</note>, must draw the expanded water from the air into itself. It is not as if water and air were themselves such a life-force for the creatures, but rather because they carry the pure light- and fire-form in their belly as a mediator of the outward nature and as the universal bread of life for all natural things. They keep this otherwise volatile **hermetic little bird** <term>hermetic little bird: a common alchemical symbol for the volatile spirit or "mercury" that must be "fixed" or captured in a medium to be utilized</term> captive, so that it can be obtained and grasped by the poor earthly creatures in the outward nature for their conservation there. Now, in the same way, you, most beloved Savior, have communicated yourself to our poor free spirit, which lies in the hunger of earthly, dark fire, because its earthly bonds, which [held] him...

...[to take] <note>completing the word from the previous page</note> in all physical things, to comprehend the elemental light and fire—the **Savior of Nature** <term>Savior of Nature: a theosophical concept where spiritual light acts as the animating and preserving force within the physical world</term>—within itself, and to conduct it to all earthly bodies of the external world, so that they may be preserved thereby in their growth and life.

Because we poor creatures, due to our earthly bonds, can grasp neither the divine light for the inner man nor the elemental light for the outer man in its spirituality and without the assistance of an external body: So let me, dearest Savior, take right deeply to heart the miserable wretchedness in which we stand according to body and spirit, as long as we are still imprisoned in this earthly hut of flesh lying under the curse. If we can no longer grasp the elemental and astral light-form in its spiritual purity without air in our earthly, dark body, even when we merely stand upon a high mountain: Oh, how would we be fit in this body to travel with you, my Savior, through all the heavens of heavens. 

Ach, how the earthliness of our bodies must first be cast off in the **putrefaction** <term>putrefaction: in this context, the alchemical process of decay and "blackening" that must precede spiritual rebirth or refinement</term> of death, and finally be led through the last transformation in a violent **resolution of fire** <term>resolution of fire: the final purification of the cosmos by fire at the end of time, melting away material impurities</term> on the Last Judgment Day, before they, dissolved from all earthly bonds, ascend to **paradisiacal clarity** <note>original: *paradiesische Klarheit*—a state of luminous, spiritual perfection and transparency</note> and may reach heaven unhindered with **Elijah** <note>a reference to the prophet Elijah being taken to heaven in a chariot of fire (2 Kings 2)</note>, even in the midst of the fire. Yet, most beloved Savior, although I daily sigh for the redemption of my body for this reason, and wish to be dissolved from these earthly bonds of my hut that weigh me down—yes, although I long to be clothed over with a **glorified body** <term>glorified body: *verklärter Leib*; the transfigured, immortal body promised to believers in the resurrection</term> as the dwelling from heaven, so that mortality may be swallowed up by immortality:

<header>❦ ) ☀️ ( ❦</header>

Yet, the **restoration** <term>restoration: *Wiederbringung*, a central concept in this type of mysticism referring to the return of all things to their original, perfect state</term> of my paradisiacal body does not lie so heavily on my heart as I am rather concerned about the transience and earthly chains of my poor spirit according to the inner man. For because this inner man also lies trapped in a dark **fire-hunger** <term>fire-hunger: a term used by theosophers like Jacob Böhme to describe a raw, unrefined desire that consumes but does not yet possess the "light" of divine love</term> for the earthly, and therefore is unable to reach You as the true heavenly light in its spirituality; indeed, because he is much too fleeting—on account of the earthly bonds with which he is surrounded—to attach himself to Your heavenly and spiritual light- and love-essence, had Your love not descended into an external, physical comprehensibility of the **Incarnation** <term>Incarnation: the act of God becoming human, literally "becoming flesh"</term>, suffering, and death: 

So we can also conclude from this how much we still lack in entering into the highest, most perfect, and most secret union with You, O heavenly pure Love, where we will no longer need the external aids of Your flesh and blood and the remembrance of Your death in the flesh for such a high union in that life to come. O, how much the poor spirit must still struggle until, clothed with Your heavenly pure light-essence as the Sun of Righteousness, it can stand in the light and fire of God, to behold Him as He is after the completion of **sanctification** <term>sanctification: the process of being made holy and purified from the "dross" of sin and worldliness</term>? 

Most beloved Lord Jesus, how many times then must we still pass through the secret way of restoration through **putrefaction** <term>putrefaction: an alchemical metaphor for the necessary decay or "death" of the old self to allow for spiritual rebirth</term> and separation, before all that is earthly is cast off from us, and we again—in the purest fire of our will’s desire, within a glorified body—grasp Your purest light- and love-essence? This is necessary to reach a complete refinement and the highest **fixation** <term>fixation: in alchemy, making a volatile substance stable and permanent; here, the soul becoming permanently "fixed" or established in God's presence</term> to stand in the light and fire of God, and after completed sanctification, to be able to behold You with an unveiled face as You are, and no longer as in a mirror or under the veil of Your external flesh.

<summary>This page explores the soul's longing to transcend its "earthly chains" and achieve a permanent, mystical union with Christ's divine light. The author uses alchemical stages like putrefaction and fixation to describe the grueling but necessary process of spiritual purification.</summary>

Finally, dearest Love, let me here consider one more important point, so that I may become truly eager to strive after this, Your **Heavenly Bread** <term>Heavenly Bread: *Himmel-Brod*, referring both to the Eucharist and the spiritual sustenance provided by Christ’s presence</term>, and also to preserve such food most carefully in the innermost depths of my heart once I have enjoyed it. So, on the other hand, You are the heavenly and eternal Savior. In Your **Light-Essence** <term>Light-Essence: *Licht-Wesen*, the spiritual substance of God's glory that illuminates the soul</term>, You serve as an imperishable and everlasting food for the fiery **will-property** <term>will-property: *Willens-Eigenschaft*, a term from Jacob Böhme's philosophy describing the inherent nature or "quality" of a person's desire or willpower</term> of our free spirit. You do this to maintain that spirit in an imperishable life, and—by separating it from all the **world-sludge** <note>original: *Welt-Schlamm*, a vivid metaphor for the corrupting influence of material desires</note> and vanity of this earthly life—to make it fixed and fire-resistant, like a **tinctured**, well-purified gold.

For You, most friendly Son of Man, have given it a food that remains into eternal life. **John 6:27**. And even if, in those final hours of our life, when we must take an extremely solemn step out of time and into eternity, the divine fire-judgments flash toward us in that terrifying moment, and as a fire-flaming sword of the **Cherub** <note>A reference to the angel placed at the gates of Eden to guard the way to the Tree of Life after the Fall</note> still guards the entrance to Paradise so that nothing unclean or common may enter therein (**Revelation 21:27**): yet this mighty fire-trial will not harm those people. Instead, in their passing through, it will make them even more beautiful and glorious—those who, in Your **Blood-Tincture** <term>Blood-Tincture: *Bluts-Tinctur*, an alchemical-theological term referring to the transformative power of Christ's blood to "tinge" or change the soul's base nature into spiritual gold</term>, have been cleansed of all the dross of the perishable world and...

You are therefore, O most penetrating power of God, to the reborn souls what a lovely kiss from the bridegroom might be to a bride burning with love. For when the benevolent and most sweet love of God in Christ Jesus embraces and kisses humanity—lifted from the dust—in the most inward union of faith and love: then you, heavenly Spirit, are the breath and sighing of His mouth, which refreshes the bride with a lovely, strengthening scent and sensation, like a poured-out ointment and balsam of life. She then becomes so beautiful and so pleasant to the Bridegroom in her finery, that He Himself must say: *Behold, my love, you are beautiful; behold, you are beautiful. ## The Fourth Meditation.
### *Concerning the Restoration of the Corrupted Creature, under the Economy of the Holy Spirit, according to the Third Principle of the Spirit.*

---

->Joel 3:1.<-

> *And after this I will pour out my Spirit*
> *upon all flesh, and your sons and daughters*
> *shall prophesy, your elders shall have dreams,*
> *and your young men shall see visions.*

Just as you, O most powerful and all-wise Spirit, are so manifold in your gifts with which you adorn your believers: so much more do I admire with holy reverence your great and infinite wisdom, in that so many thousand souls—whom you fully prepare and perfect for the Kingdom of God within this, your household—are adorned among one another with such countless varieties of gifts and spiritual powers. Yet, despite this variety, one soul always finds and recognizes in another something that it does not possess itself. And so they are indeed all brothers to one another, because according to their spiritual birth <note>original: *geiſtlichen Geburt*; the "new birth" or regeneration central to Pietist and mystical theology</note> they all have the same origin in one Spirit from the divine fire and light; but they are nevertheless distinguished from one another in infinite forms of various powers. Accordingly, *there are various gifts, but only one Spirit, and various powers, but only one GOD, who works all in all. 1 Cor. 12, 4. 6.*

...mighty **Power of God**, Lord **GOD Holy Spirit**, far be it from me to diminish your high divine majesty when I attempt to form a human and imperfect concept of you and your divine workings through representations of the qualities of the universal **Spirit of Nature**. O, your working power of grace goes infinitely higher than the power of that spirit in the realm of nature, which has received all its power to act from you yourself beforehand. But my senses are far too dim to be able to look into your infinite glory itself. Therefore, let me be content to use the **World-Spirit**—created by you—as a mirror, in which I find all your divine workings, though not enclosed within the unlimited bounds of an infinite power. 

This mighty **World-Spirit** is, as it were, the **Master Builder** <term>Master Builder: *Werckmeister*, a craftsman or architect who executes a plan; in alchemy and nature philosophy, this refers to the force that gives form to matter</term> of all natural, physical things; it is a spirit that lies hidden in the first elements from which the whole world took its origin—namely, in fire and light. It is not separated from fire and light, much less can it be made visible, but it goes forth with its working power in fire and light, and impresses all kinds of forms and shapes upon these two elementary principles <term>principles: *principiis*, the fundamental sources or active elements</term>, according to the nature of that matrix <term>matrix: *matricis*, a "womb" or surrounding substance in which something is formed or develops</term> into which this universal spirit introduces the fire and light. 

By means of such conditions, the resulting bodies are distinguished from one another in countless types and properties, as well in the **animal** as in the **vegetable** and **mineral** kingdoms. Thus, in the entire outer world, through your wonderful divine power, everything has come from the astral <term>astral: *astralischen*, referring to the celestial or "starry" influence that was believed to govern the growth and properties of things on Earth</term> fire and light; but by means of the spirit working in such principles, bodies and creatures have become so many and various that they often seem to possess entirely contrary properties to one another, which nevertheless, in the order of the general...

For after **Jacob's death**, these **twelve sons** spread out into **twelve tribes** <note>original: *Geschlechter*, meaning lineages or generations</note>, which later formed a great **military camp** or, as it were, an entire **city** in the **wilderness**. **But** this **camp** of the **twelve tribes of Israel** was once again still far from that **multitude of children** promised to **Jacob**, who were to be like the **dust** on the **earth** and break forth toward all four **corners of the world**. Here lay only **twelve tribes**, which distinguished themselves from one another by certain **lanes** or **openings**, all around a small **Tabernacle** <note>original: *Stifts-Hütte*, the portable tent-shrine used by the Israelites</note>. Yet according to **Jacob's dream**, his **seed** was to encamp like **dust** around a great **House of God** that would be similar to a **Heavenly Ladder**. Finally, after a long **time**, when the **camp** of the **twelve tribes** in the **wilderness** ceased and was, so to speak, sealed and completed, **Solomon** began to build a great **Temple**, which in some measure equaled **Jacob's Heavenly Ladder**. And here **Jacob's dream** seemed in a certain way to be fulfilled. For in **Solomon's times**, **Israel** was as numerous *as the sand by the sea. 1 Kings 4:20.* <note>original: *1 B. Kœn. 4, 20.*</note>

Revelation 7:9, 15. Oh wonderful GOD, what mysterious paths do You allow me to see here, which You have determined to walk according to Your wisdom from ancient times! ## Closing Prayer,

->*which could serve not only after the preceding*<-
->*contemplation of our precarious final times, but*<-
->*also generally and on every occasion*<-
->*for the greater awakening of the heart.*<-

O great and holy GOD, you are just, and all your <term>judgments|Gerichte; in this context, refers to divine punishments or trials sent by God to correct or test humanity</term> are right. We must confess that we have long since deserved, through our great and heaped-up sins, that you would make a complete end of us in your terrifying judgments. We already see such judgments of yours gathering over us from afar, as they prepare themselves more and more for a dangerous outbreak. But since your <term>long-suffering|Langmuth; a traditional term for God’s patient endurance and delayed anger toward sinners</term> calls us to repentance at all times: so let this counsel also not be in vain for now. Awaken in us all an unfeigned <term>repentance|Busse; refers to a sincere, transformative change of heart and turning away from sin</term>; grant that we may carefully put away from us what has hitherto been displeasing to you, and let us finally, in true faith and with a courageous heart, await that which you, dear Father, have decreed for us. Let us live in the firm assurance that wherever we may be, whether by water or by land, in foreign lands or at home, or even in rocks and clefts—yes, in every corner and wherever else your holy <term>providence|Fürsorge; the protective care and guidance of God over His creation</term> will lead us in this world during such tribulations: you could never mean us harm in the midst of such judgments. Merciful GOD, where your justice and truth do not allow these judgments of yours to be held back any longer: Oh, then may you moderate them with grace and mercy, so that we may [experience] your di—

## NOTICE <note>original: "AVERTISSEMENT"</note>.

*Behold here, God- and wisdom-loving reader, these are my **Princely Thoughts** <note>original: "Fürstliche Gedancken"</note>, which I have set down for the honor of God and my own edification, as often as I have been able to tear myself away from those most vexing courtly and other discontented affairs. Now, I wish for nothing more than that God would grant me still more grace, so that this **light of knowledge** <note>original: "Licht der Erkæntniſs"</note> ignited within me might be constantly **preserved** <note>original: "conferviret"</note> in a pure, unfeigned heart and not perhaps be transformed, through false desires of fleshly and earthly **worldly love** <note>original: "Welt-Liebe"</note>, into a flickering torch and a deceptive light <note>original: "Irrlicht"; often referred to as a "will-o'-the-wisp," symbolizing a spiritual or intellectual distraction that leads one astray</note> of a puffed-up, vain worldly wisdom. Since true **theosophical wisdom** <term>theosophical wisdom|theoſophiſche Weisheit; in this 17th/18th-century context, this refers to a direct, mystical knowledge of God and nature, distinct from modern Theosophy</term> by no means has its aim on the acquisition of great riches...*
